Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the ocean space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 370 and the convergence rate is O(284/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 370 + O(1/n^284). Summing over all modes and applying the ocean convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on ocean structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 614. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 235 with relations in degree 5.
